run/audits/batch30j_r5k_integrity_verdict_source_locator_20260508_150822/source_scans.json:352:        "preview": "def compute_integrity_verdict(\n    results: Sequence[ReplayIntegrityCheckResult],\n) -> IntegrityVerdict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Batch16-safe aggregate integrity verdict.\n\n    Rules:\n    - empty results invalid via _validate_check_results\n    - any FAIL => FAIL\n    - else any WARN => WARN\n    - else all PASS => PASS\n\n    Important:\n    Earlier Batch16 hardening forced placeholder PASS checks to FAIL. That was\n    useful as a freeze blocker, but replay_run.py now uses explicit placeholder\n    checks as its offline/static replay integrity surface. The verdict must\n    reflect actual executed check verdicts while placeholder nature remains\n    visible in check messages/details.\n    \"\"\"\n    _validate_check_results(results)\n\n    values: list[str] = []\n    for result in results:\n        verdict = getattr(result, \"verdict\", None)\n        value = getattr(verdict, \"value\", verdict)\n        label = str(value).strip().lower()\n        if label.startswith(\"integrityverdict.\"):\n            label = label.rsplit(\".\", 1)[-1].lower()\n        values.append(label)\n\n    if any(value == IntegrityVerdict.FAIL.value for value in values):\n        return IntegrityVerdict.FAIL\n    if any(value == IntegrityVerdict.WARN.value for value in values):\n        return IntegrityVerdict.WARN\n    if all(value == IntegrityVerdict.PASS.value for value in values):\n        return IntegrityVerdict.PASS\n\n    raise ReplayIntegrityValidationError(\n        f\"unknown integrity verdict value(s): {sorted(set(values))!r}\"\n    )",

run/audits/batch30j_r5bc_option_only_fut_context_compat_audit_20260510_120859/code_scans.json:1105:        "preview": "def attach_replay_dataset_readiness_verdict_to_dataset_summary(dataset_summary):\n    if not isinstance(dataset_summary, dict):\n        raise TypeError(\"dataset_summary must be a dict\")\n\n    payload = dict(dataset_summary)\n\n    declared_source_mode = payload.get(\"declared_source_mode\")\n    admissibility_ok = payload.get(\"dataset_declaration_admissibility_ok\")\n    admissibility_status = payload.get(\"dataset_declaration_admissibility_status\")\n    economics_evaluable = payload.get(\"feed_input_economics_evaluable\")\n    economics_source_status = payload.get(\"economics_source_status\")\n\n    if admissibility_status == \"no_declaration\":\n        payload[\"replay_dataset_readiness_status\"] = \"no_declaration\"\n        payload[\"replay_dataset_readiness_ok\"] = None\n        payload[\"replay_dataset_economics_comparison_ready\"] = False\n        payload[\"replay_dataset_readiness_message\"] = \"dataset declaration not present\"\n        return payload\n\n    if admissibility_ok is not True:\n        payload[\"replay_dataset_readiness_status\"] = \"rejected\"\n        payload[\"replay_dataset_readiness_ok\"] = False\n        payload[\"replay_dataset_economics_comparison_ready\"] = False\n        payload[\"replay_dataset_readiness_message\"] = payload.get(\n            \"dataset_declaration_admissibility_message\"\n        )\n        return payload\n\n    if declared_source_mode == \"quote_only_recorded\":\n        payload[\"replay_dataset_readiness_status\"] = \"admitted_quote_only\"\n        payload[\"replay_dataset_readiness_ok\"] = True\n        payload[\"replay_dataset_economics_comparison_ready\"] = False\n        payload[\"replay_dataset_readiness_message\"] = (\n            \"dataset is admissible as quote-only; economics comparison remains not ready\"\n        )\n        return payload\n\n    if declared_source_mode == \"economics_enriched_recorded\":\n        ready = (\n            economics_evaluable is True\n            and economics_source_status == \"ready\"\n        )\n        payload[\"replay_dataset_readiness_status\"] = (\n            \"admitted_economics_enriched\" if ready else \"admitted_but_not_economics_ready\"\n        )\n        payload[\"replay_dataset_readiness_ok\"] = True\n        payload[\"replay_dataset_economics_comparison_ready\"] = ready\n        payload[\"replay_dataset_readiness_message\"] = (\n            \"dataset is admissible as economics-enriched and ready for economics comparison\"\n            if ready\n            else \"dataset is admissible as economics-enriched but not yet economics-comparison ready\"\n        )\n        return payload\n\n    payload[\"replay_dataset_readiness_status\"] = \"unknown_declared_mode\"\n    payload[\"replay_dataset_readiness_ok\"] = False\n    payload[\"replay_dataset_economics_comparison_ready\"] = False\n    payload[\"replay_dataset_readiness_message\"] = (\n        f\"unsupported declared_source_mode: {declared_source_mode}\"\n    )\n    return payload"

run/audits/batch30j_r5bc_option_only_fut_context_compat_audit_20260510_120859/code_scans.json:2210:        "preview": "def compute_integrity_verdict(\n    results: Sequence[ReplayIntegrityCheckResult],\n) -> IntegrityVerdict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Batch16-safe aggregate integrity verdict.\n\n    Rules:\n    - empty results invalid via _validate_check_results\n    - any FAIL => FAIL\n    - else any WARN => WARN\n    - else all PASS => PASS\n\n    Important:\n    Earlier Batch16 hardening forced placeholder PASS checks to FAIL. That was\n    useful as a freeze blocker, but replay_run.py now uses explicit placeholder\n    checks as its offline/static replay integrity surface. The verdict must\n    reflect actual executed check verdicts while placeholder nature remains\n    visible in check messages/details.\n    \"\"\"\n    _validate_check_results(results)\n\n    values: list[str] = []\n    for result in results:\n        verdict = getattr(result, \"verdict\", None)\n        value = getattr(verdict, \"value\", verdict)\n        label = str(value).strip().lower()\n        if label.startswith(\"integrityverdict.\"):\n            label = label.rsplit(\".\", 1)[-1].lower()\n        values.append(label)\n\n    if any(value == IntegrityVerdict.FAIL.value for value in values):\n        return IntegrityVerdict.FAIL\n    if any(value == IntegrityVerdict.WARN.value for value in values):\n        return IntegrityVerdict.WARN\n    if all(value == IntegrityVerdict.PASS.value for value in values):\n        return IntegrityVerdict.PASS\n\n    raise ReplayIntegrityValidationError(\n        f\"unknown integrity verdict value(s): {sorted(set(values))!r}\"\n    )"

argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        prog=\"replay_run.py\",\n        description=\"Run one frozen replay backbone execution.\",\n    )\n\n    parser.add_argument(\"--dataset-root\", required=True, help=\"Replay dataset root directory\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--selection-mode\",\n        required=True,\n        choices=[mode.value for mode in ReplaySelectionMode],\n        help=\"Canonical replay selection mode\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--single-day\", help=\"YYYY-MM-DD for single_day / intraday_window / session_segment\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--start-date\", help=\"YYYY-MM-DD for date_range\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--end-date\", help=\"YYYY-MM-DD for date_range\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--custom-dates\", help=\"Comma-separated YYYY-MM-DD list for custom_date_list\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--weekdays\", help=\"Comma-separated weekday integers 0..6 for weekday_batch\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--months\", help=\"Comma-separated month integers 1..12 for monthly_batch\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--window-start\", help=\"HH:MM[:SS] intraday window start\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--window-end\", help=\"HH:MM[:SS] intraday window end\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--session-segment\", help=\"Named session segment for session_segment mode\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--doctrine-mode\",\n        required=True,\n        choices=[mode.value for mode in DoctrineMode],\n        help=\"locked or shadow\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--scope\",\n        required=True,\n        choices=[scope.value for scope in ReplayScope],\n        help=\"Replay topology scope\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--speed-mode\",\n        default=ReplaySpeedMode.ACCELERATED.value,\n        choices=[mode.value for mode in ReplaySpeedMode],\n        help=\"Replay clock speed mode\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--run-label\", default=None)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--experiment-profile\", default=None)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--override-pack-id\", default=None)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--dataset-id\", default=None)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--fill-model\", default=None)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--run-root\", default=None)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--required-file-stems\", default=\"\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--optional-file-stems\", default=\"\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--supported-suffixes\", default=\".jsonl,.json,.csv\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--recurse\", action=\"store_true\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--clock-start-time\",\n        default=\"2026-04-17T03:45:00Z\",\n        help=\"Replay clock start time in ISO-8601\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--channel-prefix\",\n        default=\"replay:file\",\n        help=\"Logical replay channel prefix for feed injections\",\n    )\n    return parser"
run/audits/batch30j_r5bc_option_only_fut_context_compat_audit_20260510_120859/code_scans.json:2446:        "preview": "def resolve_dataset_root(dataset_root: str, dataset_id: str | None = None) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"\n    Resolve the physical replay dataset root used by ReplayDatasetRepository.\n\n    CLI contract:\n    - --dataset-root may point directly to a dataset directory containing YYYY-MM-DD day folders.\n    - --dataset-root may also point to a parent directory when --dataset-id is supplied.\n      In that case, if dataset_root/dataset_id exists, the repository must use that child\n      path as the physical dataset root.\n\n    This keeps dataset_id as logical manifest identity while preventing selector/date\n    mismatch when materialized datasets are stored under a parent collection root.\n    \"\"\"\n    root = Path(dataset_root)\n    if dataset_id:\n        candidate = root / dataset_id\n        if candidate.exists() and candidate.is_dir():\n            return candidate\n    return root"

run/audits/batch30j_r5aw_raw_to_replay_transform_contract_audit_20260510_114216/source_scans.json:681:        "preview": "def resolve_dataset_root(dataset_root: str, dataset_id: str | None = None) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"\n    Resolve the physical replay dataset root used by ReplayDatasetRepository.\n\n    CLI contract:\n    - --dataset-root may point directly to a dataset directory containing YYYY-MM-DD day folders.\n    - --dataset-root may also point to a parent directory when --dataset-id is supplied.\n      In that case, if dataset_root/dataset_id exists, the repository must use that child\n      path as the physical dataset root.\n\n    This keeps dataset_id as logical manifest identity while preventing selector/date\n    mismatch when materialized datasets are stored under a parent collection root.\n    \"\"\"\n    root = Path(dataset_root)\n    if dataset_id:\n        candidate = root / dataset_id\n        if candidate.exists() and candidate.is_dir():\n            return candidate\n    return root"
run/audits/batch30j_r5aw_raw_to_replay_transform_contract_audit_20260510_114216/source_scans.json:938:        "context": "301:     )\n302: \n303: \n304: def resolve_dataset_root(dataset_root: str, dataset_id: str | None = None) -> Path:\n305:     \"\"\"\n306:     Resolve the physical replay dataset root used by ReplayDatasetRepository.\n307: \n308:     CLI contract:\n309:     - --dataset-root may point directly to a dataset directory containing YYYY-MM-DD day folders.\n310:     - --dataset-root may also point to a parent directory when --dataset-id is supplied.\n311:       In that case, if dataset_root/dataset_id exists, the repository must use that child",
